# Current source status — v0.4.99.2l (INTERNAL)

Updated rule: all three singular legendary pieces can be earned **before** the character first earns General/field command. Character reputation, relationships, witness testimony, and voluntary choices—not rank—control the opportunity. The existing late acquisition scenes and legacy saves remain supported for characters who do not take the early opportunity. Each is earned only once; the portable transfer remains spoiler protected in user-facing summaries. See `ALL_UNDER_HEAVEN_EARLY_LEGENDARY_STORY_AUDIT_v0.4.99.2l_INTERNAL.md` for private implementation details.

# ADDENDUM — All Under Heaven v0.4.99.2k: Narrative-Earned 8-Stage Portrait System

**Authoritative executable:** `ALL_UNDER_HEAVEN_v0.4.99.2k_DEV_EARNED_PORTRAITS.html`  
**SHA-256:** `4464bc3b041340f099b42037e189b80f5b126f1c57ab571fc9ade19b21356747`  
**Based on:** `v0.4.99.2j`, SHA-256 `7559e01a28ab3bd96d37be1dd33feb03a416c7b9fd27ca2a9f4b293adadb5ba7`  
**Status:** DEVELOPMENT; NOT BETA; preserves original narrative and state format.

This checkpoint expands the earlier five-stage concept preview to eight approved standalone visual identities: **Nobody, Soldier, Officer, General, King, King at War, Emperor, Emperor at War**. Protagonist remains male, clean-shaven, consistent Face Master Option C. No story scenes, romance paths, item IDs, save keys, rank claims or relationship mechanics were changed. Portrait is a read-only projection from current story state and does not grant titles, equipment, or a win.

**Stage gates:** Nobody until actual service; Soldier on accepted early service; Officer only after an explicitly accepted limited appointment; General only where existing social-position logic and explicit field command agree; King/Emperor only when current fully ratified titles remain active; battle variants only in explicitly enumerated personal-command sequences. Simply reaching a date or chapter does not unlock a higher stage. Counsels, delegated missions and abdication do not imply personal combat or retained sovereignty.

**Critical equipment chronology:** The approved images for General and the two sovereign battle variants visibly show three unique legendary pieces that have voluntary independent in-game acquisition gates. They are therefore displayed **only after all three are recorded as owned**; until then a NON-FINAL standard-gear field image prevents spoilers/unearned gear. This ninth embedded image is a provisional display fallback, not an approved ninth stage. All eight approved full-sized source PNGs are retained in the handoff, with optimized WebP images embedded offline in the HTML. The full-image modal preserves native proportions, while responsive thumbnails use object-fit:contain rather than crop.

**Current narrative gap:** The active story does not contain a directly controlled battlefield encounter after the valid imperial enthronement. Emperor at War is wired for future earned war contexts, **not automatically unlocked** in current scenes. New story content and QA are needed before it can appear via real gameplay. Independent-political/faction routes still need contextual background art review. Abdicated/retired appearance requires future bespoke art, not unearned old crowns.

**QA:** JS syntax PASS; 176 responsive/feature/checkpoint assertions across 320/390/768/1280 px PASS; 558 × 2 sampled scene body/choices renders PASS (1,116); nine images decoded in browser PASS; old `v0.4.99.2j` save PASS in simulated storage; 13 dev checkpoints exercised per viewport. Physical local-file close/reopen could NOT be verified: sandbox Chromium blocked file:// by administrator policy. Manual exhaustive editorial branch and physical mobile performance still pending.

# ADDENDUM — v0.4.99.2j / Dynamic Protagonist Portraits

**Active game:** `ALL_UNDER_HEAVEN_v0.4.99.2j_DEV_DYNAMIC_PROTAGONIST_PORTRAITS.html`

**Status:** DEVELOPMENT — not beta.

New implementation is a **presentation-only pilot**. The male protagonist's current visual tier is derived from earned, presently held game authority and evidence, not chapter number or age alone: Nobody, Soldier, General (command-tier visual), King, Emperor. Rank transitions use existing history and sovereignty predicates; this feature does not grant rank. Do not display the King/Emperor portrait on an unratified title. A peaceful abdication must not imply present imperial/royal office.

**Identity and continuity:** same face throughout, clean-shaven, hair may follow office and dress, physical maturation from age sixteen until adulthood; Timeless Heroes life rules remain unchanged. Contextual backgrounds, in-game inventory-accurate armor/robes, injury variants, and individually redrawn portrait detail remain *future art work*; the current five images are **crops of the approved 5-stage concept sheet**, not final character art. Stage I presently may look older than sixteen. On-screen captions explicitly say *Portrait preview*.

**UI:** Character page shows a clickable portrait on desktop and mobile; the existing full-image modal now accepts the protagonist as well as encountered NPCs. Zoom buttons (+/−), Escape, backdrop click and close button work. Previously unencountered NPC portrait security unchanged. All portrait images are embedded as WebP in the standalone HTML; no new save keys or relationship data.

**QA:** 4 viewports; 64 passed checks including stage gates, zoom, modal behavior and hidden NPC portrait; 1,116 scene renders over two sampled states with valid choice destinations; five images decoded; 13 developer checkpoint enter/exit checks left stored save bytes identical; old v0.4.99.2i save loaded in simulated storage. ## Player-facing People interface (12 November 2026)
- User-approved categories: **Male → Friends, Neutral, Enemies**; **Female → Acquaintances, Friends, Lovers**. These are mutually exclusive view filters, not changes to character identity, permanent reputation, relationship mechanics or NPC agency.
- All **53** people remain in the registry: seven currently mapped as female in this implementation (`shen`, `linyue`, `heqing`, `caizhen`, `lan`, `songwan`, `yaoqing`); all other existing registered entries male. Historical cast is still 39 / original 14. This is a current roster registry, not a fixed rule preventing new characters.

- Female **Acquaintances**: a directly met woman without confirmed friendship or reciprocal romantic relationship. Female **Friends**: witnessed, explicitly chosen friendship or accepted Inner Circle membership. Female **Lovers**: reciprocal adult romance milestones or chosen marriage, not an expression of interest, a flirt, favorable attributes or a potential future candidate.
- Most recent explicit 230/222/217 relationship decision supersedes an earlier relationship or separation (marriage remains a valid partner status). Female antagonism remains possible in the **internal state and plot**; it is not suppressed or converted into romance. Interface preference alone does not rewrite NPC loyalty or story decisions.
- Male filtering preserves inherited Friends/Neutral/Enemies categorization for compatibility. Its former generic shared-council fallback is a separate *future* quality audit; no core relationship logic was rewritten in this UI-only patch.
- Current counts: 558 scenes / 12 chapters / 53 characters / 14 portraits. Save keys, schemas, scene code and romance consent requirements unchanged. Nothing in this patch is a new story encounter.

## Targeted QA and limitations
- JS parsing PASS; four browser widths 320/390/768/1280 with no page errors or horizontal overflow; gender toggles and 3 relationship tabs usable. Synthetic state checks cover unencountered shielding, friendship, intimacy, spouses, later friendship/parting, and retention of ordinary encounter flags.
- Simulated old-save load PASS; **13/13** Developer Checkpoint enter/exit tests kept persisted save unchanged; **1,116** sample scene body + choice-target checks PASS. These are automated selected-state checks, not complete route editorial validation.
- Physical `file://` close/reopen storage durability and manual branch-wide reading remain untested; historical source audits (27 pending), portrait backlog (39), audio and beta gates remain as in v1.99.2h.

## HC04 · Historical chronology and independent archives

- **All prior content retained:** 53 registered persons (39 historical; 14 original), 12 chapters, 556 inherited scenes plus two optional interlude scenes = **558**; the prior v0.4.99.2f HTML remains the direct ancestor, with the same state fields, NPC flags and save storage keys.
- **Dated historical roles:** HC01/HC02 profile fields that previously appeared as timeless role titles are now labeled `Recorded Role · 190 CE` and `Recorded Role · 200 CE` / `Recorded Role · 208 CE`. They are immutable historical observations, not assertions about the official's current employment or survival. People visibility, personal meeting flags and undisclosed personality rules remain unchanged.
- **Historical reference layer:** HC04 includes twelve **developer-only** candidate baseline end-year annotations for source comparison, with incomplete/contested dates deliberately omitted. These are NOT forced deaths or in-game canonical outcomes. Divergent histories remain possible. Research pointers: Rafe de Crespigny, *A Biographical Dictionary of Later Han to the Three Kingdoms* (Brill, 2007), Australian National University repository https://dspace-prod.anu.edu.au/items/372d4ef8-9703-4d97-9f1c-addc52c5313c; compare primary *Sanguozhi* for individual entries before certifying a year. This is an indexed *preliminary* chronology review, not a full 39-person source-verified biography audit.
- **Interlude at 214 CE:** accessible only when the player actually preserved an optional northern dispatch report in 200 CE (`hc02_200_note`). An old report is compared to a later dated account. Players may keep separate sources, investigate changed roads or leave the file undecided. No in-person meeting or unearned loyalty is created. Source route remains available to Chapter X's original choices.
- **Interlude at 220 CE:** accessible only after the 214 interlude was completed. The current archive keeper has different jurisdiction; player choices emphasize verified sources or independent offices rather than presuming any officer alive. Rejoins all original Chapter XI choices. The interludes never appear if their predecessor events were not experienced.
- **Developer-only chronology checks:** source provenance, outcome enums, under-year flags and chronology order; advisory, no rewriting of player history, no new save schema. Ordinary player UI continues to hide internal chronology table and undiscovered NPC identities.
- **Visual status unchanged:** 8 of 53 embedded portraits. Keep the historical-heroic UI, the single labeled hamburger menu on desktop and mobile, and English-only title. New art/audio is planned after this chronology pass, not falsely reported as complete.

## Verified HC04 QA scope

- 18 complete 214/220 optional-branch combinations across 390px and 1280px browser viewports, 3,348 dynamic scene render checks in six total snapshots, one source-legal route across 135 choices from Chapter VII into Chapter XI, 13 developer checkpoints preserving main save, and v0.4.99.2f save migration in simulated browser storage passed. ## HC03 — Living Historical Characters: repeat encounters and persistent records

- **Roster:** 53 unique registered characters (39 historical, 14 original), unchanged from HC02. **Scene registry:** 556 (553 inherited plus 3 additive new scenes). All 12 chapters preserved; opening age 16, year 190 CE. The entire previous HTML, assets, legacy flags and NPC IDs remain. Do not count HC03 as new NPC recruitment.
- A voluntary follow-up is possible around 210 CE, only if the player actually completed the HC02 river-register episode in 208 CE. If the player chose a direct-meeting path with the relevant southern officer, an **optional** second audience may be requested; otherwise a dated report can be filed through clerks, but no personal meeting or familiarity is fabricated.
- The 210 encounter depends on both a specific eyewitness `historical_meet_*` flag AND the compatible HC02 selected branch. Player may offer bounded evidence, commission renewed verification, seek unsupported authority (rejected), use the office without a personal audience or leave its affairs alone. There is no new automatic favor, trust, rank, army, item, horse, coin, alliance, marriage, or ending.
- Three new scenes: `ch09_hc03_01_records_two_years_on`, `ch09_hc03_02_officers_return`, `ch09_hc03_03_no_simple_seal`. Existing `ch09_01` options remain unchanged, with one optional additional choice when its prerequisite was truly played. New content returns to original `lc81_210_shared_table` choice sequence.
- **Persistent, conditional readbacks** in existing `ch10_01` (214 CE) and `ch11_01` (later political period): the old river records reflect precisely what the player did. They are letters and documents, **not fictitious later face-to-face meetings** with a character whose death or office status may have changed.
- For previously met relevant NPCs, `npcMemories`, `npcObservations` and `npcFacts` acquire 210 CE follow-up information ONLY when personal conversation occurred. Unmet characters and report-only readers remain invisible in ordinary People. Dev Tools have an extra targeted chronology/memory warning summary; it does not publish future facts in ordinary play.
- NPCs continue operating under independent commands in the authored narrative. **This is scripted autonomy and record continuity, not an exhaustive world-state simulation or complete historical lifespan model.** Geography, disputed sources and authority limits remain important.
- Current portrait coverage: **8/53 registered NPCs have embedded portrait entries**, 45 remain to be illustrated, reviewed or intentionally left as initials. Portrait production waits for the agreed visual pass; no new portrait assets added here.
- Test evidence: 16 targeted follow-up variants at mobile/desktop, one actually traversed 208→210 playable route, earlier HC02 branches preserved, a v0.4.99.2e prior-save restore, 13 developer checkpoints with storage isolation, and 3,336 dynamic render/choice checks over six sample states. These checks are **bounded**; do not claim exhaustive 190–250 NPC lifespan, every narrative scene or physical `file://` durability.

- Deeper cross-era NPC office, survival, faction autonomy and relationship divergence throughout alternate 190–250 outcomes.
- Full human editorial review and source-legal reachability beyond sampled states, all ending families, and physical user-device close/reopen save validation.
- 45 absent NPC portraits and art consistency; visual/UI/audio work begins only after agreed NPC-depth milestone closure.

# ACTIVE WORLD BIBLE — v1.99 FAST / EDITORIAL & BRANCH QA CHECKPOINT

**Active executable:** `ALL_UNDER_HEAVEN_v0.4.99_DEV_EDITORIAL_BRANCH_QA.html` (SHA-256 `229c4cc273af355c4272658ba5c979549a649ab667403e54acdbbecddc8addd3`). **Narrative canon and character roster remain unchanged except for small editorial corrections.** 539 scenes, 29 People, 12 chapters, three main terminal families plus eight retained historical manuscript entries. This current-first overlay supersedes technical status in older headings below, while retaining their canon and prior source context.

## Canon and editorial constraints

- The player begins as an unknown 16-year-old in 190 CE and shapes their own history; historical people retain independent choice and agency. Rankings, kingdoms, military victories, spouses, children and legacies are earned, never automatically granted by a title.
- Six romance paths, voluntary 18+ consensual non-graphic adult intimacy and family decisions, young/adult children's independent ambitions, enduring Inner Circle and merit-appointed generals remain. Do not expose private gauges or hidden causes in the UI.
- Exactly one earned great glaive, one earned new white-silver armor set and one earned, player-named exceptional white horse; hidden names and acquisition events stay internal, as do narrative surprises and major loyalty/death conditions.
- Coins support housing, land, facility choices only; no added commodity markets or mandatory taxes. War can be fought personally or directed, but always with costs, logistics, witnesses and the possibility of failure.
- English interface/narrative; Indonesian spoiler-free progress reports. Save slot names and version-1 save schema stay compatible, with extra safeguards against corrupt imports.


## v0.4.99 editorial/branch technical findings

- All 539 scene bodies and choices were invoked under 33 diverse states; no rendering exceptions or invalid choice destinations were found within those contexts. 17,787 HTML fragments passed the targeted tag/placeholder lint after correcting three unclosed paragraphs and two nested markup compositions.
- 120 legal randomized lifetime routes finish successfully (34,103 decisions); 367 unique scene IDs and 457 distinct executed edges reached. The 172 not reached are still subject to directed/manual review; non-visit is not proof of dead content.
- One witness-noun continuity correction and a defensive fallback for an incomplete historic family visit prevent an undefined-year deep link without manufacturing relationships or changing legal romance options.
- The two guided lawful paths (420/422 decisions) still reach a previously established earned long-lived terminal route. Archived eight lifetime manuscripts, core plot choices, 29 characters, all image resources and save keys remain.
- Native standalone file-origin close/reopen persistence and full human editorial sign-off are outstanding gates. **Development checkpoint only; no beta/final approval.**

# ACTIVE WORLD BIBLE — v1.98 FAST / OFFLINE STABILITY & UX

**Current authority:** `ALL_UNDER_HEAVEN_v0.4.98_DEV_OFFLINE_STABILITY_UX.html` · SHA-256 `b2068ec5883984b9b7a9f17e9ab832ec8b8e8700fa8ce3cc71babc106e992d4e`. 539 scenes, 29 People, 12 chapters and three main ending families. ## Source-of-truth invariants (player report spoiler-safe)

- The protagonist begins as a 16-year-old unknown in 190 CE and earns influence, authority and any martial achievements by demonstrated decisions; historical people pursue their own lives regardless of the player's presence.
- The same independent NPC memory, Inner Circle, earned Five Dragon Generals, voluntary adult family options, six differentiated romance routes and three main ending families remain. No retroactive spouse/child/heir, no automatic death/romance/advancement. Adult intimacy remains consensual, metaphorical and non-graphic.
- Keep the single earned Great Glaive, single newly forged white-silver armor and single earned, player-named white horse under existing hidden prerequisites. Never include names, givers, grant scenes or secret ending conditions in public QA/development reports.
- Coins only fund lightweight house, land and facilities; no added tax engine or purchasable legendary power. Every prior 539 scene body and option function, 29 People entries and eight archived endings are byte-equivalent in source function representation to v0.4.97.

## v0.4.98 continuity and storage safeguards

- Same canonical and legacy save keys, original save version 1 and `resolve` migration path. Separate campaign and sandbox slots continue. A recovery copy of unreadable local storage can be downloaded without silently deleting its original bytes.
- The game confirms each local storage write with immediate readback; errors trigger a visible warning and an export path rather than a false 'saved' confirmation. Save on decision/manual save, plus pagehide/visibility checkpoint where supported. QA preview still never writes to canonical saves.
- Invalid, oversized or failed imports do not switch the user's active campaign. A successful import updates the selected corresponding slot only after validation and a user's overwrite approval. When a corrupt current slot is detected and a legacy save is available, automatic saves do not erase the corrupt slot. Replacing it requires an explicit user-directed new start or import.
- Keyboard focus visibility and menu focus handling improved; no changes to narrative choices, NPC registrations, story progression, gates or equipment. Portraits and all embedded imagery remain in the HTML itself, with no external runtime dependencies detected.

## Limitations / gates

Native physical `file://` localStorage behavior after browser closure/restart remains unverified because the test environment blocks direct browser navigation. Injected browser storage tests do not satisfy this gate. The full human editorial review of 539 scenes and all branches remains a distinct v0.4.99 milestone. No beta declaration.

## War consequence design — implemented, spoiler-free

Three *optional* aftermath encounters at the recorded results of the Northern, Western, and Eastern campaigns. Only the real earned King with a genuinely contested front, in the proper campaign year, encounters the aftermath. The player must decide how to treat missing troops, harmed civilians, or retreating survivors. This never converts the failed front into a victory, cedes foreign territory, invents an alliance, or forces an imperial title. Consequences are written to Journal and recalled in the regional account and all three ending families. Older ending sources remain unchanged.

## Signature gear — implemented, spoiler-free

Two optional and singular narrative recognitions are now playable on established public-service routes. One is a **powerful legacy Great Glaive**; the other is **an entirely new white-silver scale armor**, not a recolor or improvement of the starting attire. Each demands independently witnessed prior history and a meaningful optional acceptance; a player can decline without breaking the main story. Neither item can be bought with Coins, copied by normal choice mechanics, or used to bypass real battle prerequisites. If owned, each permits a distinctive tactical choice in an existing front, still resolved through the original supply/proof rules. The exact names, gift-givers, scenes, and hidden eligibility conditions are **not disclosed here**; learn them by playing.

The previously established white horse remains the same named, optionally earned, long-lived companion after the unranked opening. No new mount or replacement was introduced. Coins continue to fund owned property improvements and original minor social obligations rather than a legendary equipment shop.

Roster may remain 0–5, officers may be individually relieved and later reconsidered, and the King may first found/review the standards during the optional 239 CE northern campaign. A service-unproven Lin Shuo refuses an unsupported royal rank. Five Dragon Generals are **separate from the seven-person Inner Circle**, yet members may overlap. Royal mandates do not grant another faction's allegiance, force victories, turn Wei Zhen into a military general, or bar protagonist frontline service. People UI displays only a formally founded and earned roster. The seven Inner Circle slots and Grand Strategist are unaffected.

**Source-legal organic QA from 190:** 5 independent mechanically traversed directed routes: King with 5 standards; King with 2; King with zero; King declining founding; non-King denied the military order. The played 5-standard route reaches the already-existing 250 CE E01 epilogue through **307 choices**, with no injected title, personnel, or territory. This test is **choice-engine simulation, not a full manual UI readthrough**. Additional source-consistent 234 fixtures check 0–5 appointments, independent refusal, 239 late founding, release, legacy save normalization and permission. Chromium UI click tests at 390px/1280px passed. 8 original ending manuscripts retained.

**QA evidence:** 1,312 focused v0.4.82 checks; 6,284 legacy v0.4.81 checks; 518 structural checks; syntax PASS; five legal 190-to-terminal route variants; 390/1280 browser clicks PASS. **NOT certified:** literal `file://` persistent save/reload, manual read of every ending, long-term historical recruitability, full independent-NPC career and potential on-screen battle casualties, three-ending migration or imperial abdication. ## LATEST USER-APPROVED ENDING & PEACEFUL SUCCESSION DECISION — 11 NOVEMBER 2026

**Authority / status: LOCKED FUTURE DESIGN. NOT YET CODED.** This section supersedes earlier proposals for eight separate *future* endings, five grouped endings, contested heirs, and 280 CE as a required finish. It does **not** delete or override anything already executable in HTML v0.4.79. Preserve all eight original E01–E08 manuscripts, ending conditions, save IDs and existing option routes until a separately tested migration exists. This is an approved **target architecture**, not a claim that the game now has three endings.

- **THREE main ending families (target):** (1) **The Sovereign** — become King, possibly unite China and become Emperor; branches include remain King, reign as Emperor, or voluntarily abdicate *after* successful unification and a meaningful (non-obligatory) reign. (2) **The Legendary General** — heroic military legacy without needing a sovereign throne; can continue command, retire peacefully, or hand over command. (3) **The Final Legacy** — premature death / fatal consequences of meaningful choices; a serious potential outcome, never forced merely for variety. Past government/reform, independent domain, personal/family, and peaceful retirement conclusions become **conditional epilogue variants**, not additional major ending families. Diplomacy and civil administration remain substantial gameplay.
- **PEACEFUL ABDICATION IS A VOLUNTARY E01 VARIANT, NOT AN EXTRA ENDING.** It requires that the protagonist *actually* became Emperor of unified China and has an **adult, demonstrably capable, eligible child chosen by the player**, not just the firstborn or a hidden automatic successor. Biological or legally recognized adopted children may be eligible when game relationship and legal context supports it. A credible successor should have earned preparation/experience, demonstrated judgment and public-administrative responsibility; the selection and abdication are explicit player decisions. If no appropriate adult child exists, this specific abdication-to-child option is unavailable; never conjure a spouse, child or heir to unlock it. Timeless Heroes applies: neither abdication nor successor readiness requires an aging/illness decline.
- **NO SUCCESSION CONFLICT.** Explicit user veto: **do not write inheritance fights, sibling contests, throne-grabbing intrigues, rival heir civil war, a disputed designated heir or a compulsory succession crisis** as the price of the abdication route. The selected heir is genuinely worthy; succession is deliberate, respected and peaceful. Appropriate challenges of *governance*, reconstruction and leadership after abdication may remain, without secretly reintroducing an heir dispute. Other children retain agency and dignified lives, not automatic antagonists.
- **PLAYER AGENCY & PACING:** Do not force Emperor, abdication, marriage, children, or any single ending. Prefer a focused conclusion around 245–250 CE as an authoring target, not a hard calendar gate; King-before-optional Five Dragon Generals-before-optional Emperor order persists. Allow strong military play throughout and no mandatory long retirement arc. The 12-chapter structure is retained; Chapter XII may contain several optional late campaign phases.
- **QA/migration pending:** design a mapping of existing E01–E08 runtime outcomes to three target families without deleting prose, losing route flavor or breaking saves. Validate earned family/adult heir, childless emperor, regional King, continuing Emperor, Legendary General, fatal branches, age/calendar and UI before claiming the migration complete. Never claim this addendum proves executable abdication or three-ending architecture.

## WB-01Q — POST-v0.4.79 USER-APPROVED DESIGN / NEXT-CHAT HANDOFF

## LOCKED USER DESIGN DECISIONS — 11 NOVEMBER 2026 — IMPLEMENTATION PENDING

**Status = APPROVED_DESIGN, NOT_IMPLEMENTED in HTML v0.4.79.** These are explicit decisions made after the v0.4.79 executable checkpoint, not functions/flags/scenes already in that build. Later chats MUST read this section before changing the age system, early Inner Circle formation, NPC cast, ruler path, military honors or family timeline. User-facing updates must not claim QA or implementation until code is changed and tested. English playable narration/UI; Indonesian development reports. The player remains the protagonist and sole decision-maker of their life.

### A. UNIVERSAL TIMELESS HEROES / YOUTHFUL APPEARANCE (user confirmed)

- **Intent:** the protagonist and beloved/favorite NPCs should visibly remain young throughout the campaign, including the long campaign to unify China. To avoid an arbitrary favored-only exception, **apply the same fictional life-course rule to all humans in the world**, historical and original, men and women; this is a literary convention, not a supernatural power, scientific proposition or new claim about actual Three Kingdoms history.
- **Clock stays real:** world calendar, elapsed years, birthdays and **chronological ages** keep advancing. New protagonist starts at **16 in 190 CE** (unchanged), is 60 in 234, and may be 76 in 250; do NOT freeze the stored age, reset age on scene entry, break year/age sync, or rewrite saved character age. Real historical birth-year chronology, relative seniority, rank, meeting feasibility, and known deadlines remain meaningful. Existing saves preserve old data.
- **Growth and youth:** children and adolescents develop normally until adult maturity. Once an individual reaches their established **adult appearance**, age-related visible aging and routine physiological decline cease; adults retain youthful adult looks despite increasing chronological age. No fixed numeric maturation threshold is invented for every person; preserve the individual age, visual state, and the romance-specific anchors below. Individuals already elderly when introduced are **not** retroactively rejuvenated. Facial expressions, scars, injuries, illness, disability, style and the consequences of lived experience can still change.
- **Mortality remains:** injury, battle, disease, murder and consequential story events can kill characters. Mere old chronological age should NOT automatically trigger a death, mandatory retirement or weakened combat ability under the new system. No invulnerability, forced resurrection, immortality or plot armor; senior advisors and veteran generals remain autonomous. This rule still needs a focused audit of existing age-driven scenes, character descriptions, mortality gates, and ending prose before code implementation.
- **Family consistency:** children still grow to adulthood and can develop independence, adult careers and optional succession stories. Parenthood/pregnancy/adoption remain elective with recorded consent and actual played decisions; never auto-award spouses, heirs or property. The protagonist's youth does not entitle them to romantic access to anyone.
- **Adult-romance preservation:** Six fictional adult romance candidates retain their v0.4.70 **stable adult age anchors**: Shen Lian 29; Lin Yue 26; He Qing 28; Cai Zhen 27; Lan 25; Song Wan 30. These were already deliberately non-aging **for the six candidates only**. Universal youth extends adult visual preservation to the rest of the cast but MUST NOT alter the six approved anchor ages, eligibility, mutually consenting adult-only romance or marriage constraints. No intimate/romantic content involving minors; adults age 18+ only. Treat historical NPC recorded birth years separately as chronological facts, not frozen identity ages.
- **Presentation:** avoid continual lines about miraculous anti-aging or characters being frail due only to calendar age. Experienced characters can acquire gravitas, scars, changing habits and political judgment while remaining physically youthful adults. Keep occasional birthday events and aging-related respect in social terms, without describing physical decrepitude based on chronological years alone.

### B. PLAYER-BUILT INNER CIRCLE (user confirmed; primary strategist clarified in B1)

- **Max 7 active trusted Inner Circle members**, separate from the potentially very large wider military/civil court and distinct from friendship count. Membership is earned and changeable, never automatically filled. A person need not hold a formal office to be close to the protagonist. The seven desired functional contributions are: closest companion, grand marshal, grand strategist, elite general, chancellor, logistics commander, and diplomat/intelligence chief. **Roles are descriptive, not seven mandatory exclusive slots or automatic rank awards.**
- **11 principal candidates planned:** four existing original NPCs **Han Sheng, Xu Wen, Qin Rui, Deng Yu**; **three new original candidates not yet named or designed in code** (strategist, diplomatic/intelligence talent, rising field officer); four higher-priority historical possibilities **Zhao Yun, Xu Shu, Mi Zhu, Lu Su**. These are design candidates, NOT currently recruited/guaranteed, and their whereabouts, dates, oaths, lives and loyalties must be respected. 3–4 opportunities for substantial lifelong friendship. Other historical figures, including Liu Bei's celebrated retainers, may become credible alternative-history allies only if hard-earned; do not steal loyalists by fiat.
- Every member has independent beliefs, friends, rivals, ambitions, possible family, refusal/departure/betrayal/death and world actions. Members have relationships **with one another**, not only with the protagonist. Avoid spoiler/exposed hidden personalities. Reward actual shared history, not just combat rank or hidden score.

### B0. EARLY INNER CIRCLE GENESIS — 190 CE ONWARD (explicit user decision, 11 Nov 2026)

**MANDATORY START:** The *formation process* of the player's future Inner Circle **must begin in the opening 190 CE playable story (Prologue / earliest Chapter I)**, while the protagonist is age 16 and before receiving a lordship, army, royal crown, or formal council. This overrides any earlier shorthand suggesting council recruitment begins only when the protagonist becomes a ruler. It **does not** mean seven preselected companions or a court exist on the first screen.

- **Immediately encounter possible lifelong companions** through location-plausible shared work, journeys, military service or crises. Author real choices to help, trust, disagree with, distance from, or lose the earliest people; a first meeting may be just an acquaintance. An initial circle of **0–2 organically trusted people** is a pacing target, not a free grant, forced minimum, or automatic entourage. Entry can happen when warranted after shared action, even before any formal title.
- **Reuse established early-character continuity where feasible** (especially Han Sheng and Xu Wen if the actual source route, place, and timeline permit). Qin Rui and Deng Yu can join later based on genuine witnessed meetings, not forced 190 appearance. Do not retroactively add historical figures who were elsewhere, alive at different stages, or loyal to other patrons. Existing NPC identities and discoveries remain untouched until played.
- **The original Grand Strategist candidate must have an early, meaningful introduction and an attainable friendship/recruitment pathway beginning in Act I (190–192), or as soon as local travel/story feasibility genuinely allows—not hidden until kingship or late empire.** Early candidate may be a young scholar/learner with notable judgment, *not* magically fully accomplished at the first encounter; earned counsel, mentoring, mistakes and accomplishments support later formal appointment. If the early encounter is declined, provide conditional later re-encounter(s) rather than compulsory recruitment. Preserve exactly one primary Grand Strategist at a time, a possible empty seat, and historically plausible optional alternatives.
- **Development stages**: 190–192 acquaintance and first trusts → 193–199 repeated companionship, disagreement and first earned stable fellowship → 200 onward earned military/political responsibilities, personal change and potential departures → regional rule, kingship and a larger court, with no automatic seven-member completion → Five Dragon Generals only *after becoming King* and separately from Inner Circle → potential imperial unification without rewriting early relationships.
- **Show long-memory callbacks** in choices, visits, council debates, frontline missions and family life: longstanding bonds affect later offers and reactions without replacing player agency. Members may retain friendships after dismissal, be close without holding office, or accept high office without being personally close. Also author inter-member connections, jealousies and loyalties. Player can choose solitude or refuse candidates; the game still progresses.
- **Do not conflate with starting power**: protagonist at 16 in 190 CE has no free permanent entourage, no Grand Strategist already in post, no Five Dragon honors, no royal authority. Formative closeness is *personal*, not an early government cabinet. Maintain privacy of NPC personality/hidden gauges.
- **Implementation/QA NOT YET DONE in v0.4.79 HTML**: inspect actual Prologue/Chapter I inbound choices and NPC chronology; insert small optional encounter/choice arcs, preserve existing choice effects and one-time flags; ensure age/time, no retroactive historical discovery, save compatibility, refuse/leave/rejoin alternatives, later council callbacks, no softlock and no forced seven members. Run targeted early and late regressions, including early Grand Strategist introduction and an optional zero-Inner-Circle path. Do NOT claim this is already playable merely because World Bible specifies it.

### B1. GUARANTEED GRAND STRATEGIST OPPORTUNITY / PRIMARY STRATEGIST (user confirmed, 11 Nov 2026)

- **Binding narrative requirement:** among the protagonist's maximum **seven Inner Circle members**, the intended functioning council **must have one named primary Grand Strategist**: a genuine strategic mind of a caliber comparable in *narrative importance and versatility* to **Zhuge Liang or Sima Yi**, not a literal copy or a character with guaranteed foresight. This is an additional explicit player decision, taking priority over the earlier statement that all seven role descriptions are optional. **Exactly one primary strategic adviser at a time** may hold this council responsibility; another adviser may exist outside the Inner Circle. Do not expand the seven-member cap.
- **Do not auto-recruit.** Make at least one credible **original Grand Strategist candidate** available through story-grounded encounters and choices, independent of the survival/recruitability of any historical figure. Recruitability must be a real playable opportunity (not merely a codex mention), and advancement into the Inner Circle should require mutual trust, demonstrated competence, and willing acceptance. If the player repeatedly rejects every candidate or the strategist leaves/dies, the role may temporarily or permanently remain vacant by player consequence. Hence guarantee **an attainable opportunity**, not guaranteed companionship against the player's decisions.
- **Strong distinct character:** independent ideas, limitations, human motives and substantial relationships with the protagonist and other NPCs. Can propose multiyear grand strategy, assess military logistics, predict reasonable opponent moves, coordinate battlefield operations with the Five Dragon Generals, conduct diplomacy and institutional reform, and **challenge the protagonist's plans** when warranted. Avoid infallible clairvoyance or NPC usurping the player's leadership. Meaningful disagreement, mistakes, loyalty strain and rivalries have lasting outcomes.
- **Recruitment alternatives:** a historically plausible alternate-history **Xu Shu** may compete for the primary adviser role if legitimately recruited; **Zhuge Liang** or **Sima Yi** may only be recruited through exceptional, historically coherent alternative-history branches that respect existing obligations, age, place, factions and relationship history. No historically famous adviser is automatically reassigned to the protagonist. A candidate's arrival must never retcon a prior NPC's loyalty.
- **King → Emperor continuity:** if the protagonist establishes a kingdom, the primary Grand Strategist should be a true actor in strategic deliberations **before** the unification campaign and during it, able to disagree with the five independently selected Dragon Generals. After unification, retain independent statesmanship, fallibility, and political risk. The protagonist makes the final player choice and can personally lead armies.
- **Coding and QA obligation:** implement original candidate introduction and organic recruitment, show one clear primary Grand Strategist assignment, unique seat validation, vacancies/replacements, and callbacks into military/political decisions; test early successful recruitment, deliberate refusal, death/departure, eligible historical alternate, and coexistence with the seven-person limit and five Dragon Generals. **NOT IMPLEMENTED in v0.4.79 HTML.**

### C. KING → FIVE DRAGON GENERALS → UNIFICATION → EMPEROR (user confirmed)

- **Progression is optional and earned:** young protagonist → independent regional ruler → **King** → option to formally establish **Five Dragon Generals** (*Five Dragon Generals*, fictional `五龍將`, Wǔ Lóng Jiàng) **while already King, BEFORE becoming Emperor** → actual military/diplomatic multi-region unification → optional imperial oath/coronation as **Emperor of a unified China**. May stay King, fail at unity, refuse emperor title or pursue other ending paths. The v0.4.79 HTML **already implements an earned regional crown and earned imperial unification variant of E01**, but DOES NOT YET implement Five Dragon Generals or the fuller Inner Circle system.
- **Max 5 Dragon Generals, not required to fill all five.** Award to meritorious original or historical serving commanders via credible kingly authority, player approval and a meaningful ceremony. Titles should recognize different strengths (large-force leadership, valor, tactical command, defense, independent campaign) without pigeonholing permanent abilities. The cohort is a fictional honour, **not** the historical office or a rename of Liu Bei's literary Five Tigers. The earlier suggestion **Five Pillars of the Realm is superseded** by the user's chosen name **Five Dragon Generals**.
- Dragon membership and 7-person Inner Circle are **independent, overlapping sets**: a commander may be on both; Dragon status alone does not mean private friendship or automatic obedience. Appointments have social consequences for selected/unselected officers. Resignations, deaths, unworthiness or defection may leave vacancies; fame and accomplishments affect later campaigns. The protagonist still personally leads battles if chosen and credible. After imperial unification, surviving Dragons can retain honor and obtain new earned responsibilities without being auto-promoted.
- This king-founded Dragon institution is **distinct from** the older optional 'Six Tiger Generals' honor proposed for an exceptional Liu Bei/Shu-aligned literary alternate-history route; do not conflate the two, do not make the Five Tigers historically official, and do not award either by default.

### D. KINGDOM-INSPIRED UNIFICATION CAMPAIGN — APPROVED CREATIVE DIRECTION (11 NOV 2026)

**BINDING USER DIRECTION:** The protagonist's optional progression from regional King to unifier and Emperor should draw **high-level dramatic and structural inspiration from Yasuhisa Hara’s manga *Kingdom***. This is inspiration in campaign *scale*, escalating military leadership, strategic interplay, complex rival commanders, royal politics and the emotional cost of conquest. Do **NOT** copy manga arcs, named fictional units, invented manga dialogue, scenes, character designs or exclusive character relationships. The *Kingdom* story concerns the Qin/Warring States period, **not** the 190 CE Three Kingdoms setting. *All Under Heaven* continues using its own alternate chronology, active historical agents, cast, cities and 12-chapter structure; *Romance of the Three Kingdoms* remains an additional literary inspiration, not mandatory railroaded history. Publisher premise source (general only): https://www.viz.com/kingdom ; anime premise/background: https://www.kingdom-anime.com/introduction/ . No plot spoilers from those works required.

**Campaign framework (future writing/design; no new code yet):**
- **Early cast to late dynasty:** relationships and skills forged from 190 CE should substantively matter in the post-kingship campaigns. Original Grand Strategist develops organically and may later become a vital but *fallible* war planner. Inner Circle stays max 7, membership earned. Five Dragon Generals is a **King-founded** (not Emperor-founded) honor of up to five earned commanders, with credible appointment, own armed forces or assignments when warranted, competing counsel, personal costs and the possibility of losses or defections. Overlap with Inner Circle allowed, but not forced.
- **Multiple connected operations, not one-click conquest:** after King status, organize Northern / Western / Jiangdong theaters according to the **existing v0.4.79 state map**, each with distinct map constraints, supply and movement, rival sovereign interests, independent field commanders, sieges/strongholds, diplomacy, local legitimacy and post-battle governance. Military operations may involve deception, reconnaissance, food supply, route choices, strategic withdrawals, morale and difficult alternatives. Use historically/geographically plausible travel and actors; no unearned command over their factions. Win/loss must follow actual flags and choices; avoid adding a real-time RTS or assuming deterministic success. One chapter may contain a long campaign arc; **do not add a 13th chapter**.
- **Meaningful war councils:** show direct conflict between Grand Strategist, Dragon Generals, civilian ministers and protagonist; each has motives and information limits. Let player pick between plausible strategies, including personal frontline command. Rival kings/generals react independently and can foil plans. Each major theater should produce delayed effects on friends, soldiers, household, population, borders, treasury, logistics and later treaties; defeat/retreat/compromise remain meaningful.
- **Victories cost something:** conquered regions require food, legal institutions, civilian protection and acceptance. A campaign may achieve military supremacy but fail political unification. Main character may remain a regional King, abandon expansion, face failed campaigns, choose a non-imperial ending, or earn *actual* all-region political and military settlement and **optionally** accept imperial coronation. Only the earned all-region path may use 'Emperor of a unified China' wording.
- **No historical determinism:** Red Cliffs remains a major earlier climax, and historical protagonists/commanders can respond independently. Do not turn every historical ruler into a scripted boss, erase 190–234 achievements, guarantee survival, force a marriage/heir, or solve military history with one combat duel. Rulers and advisors have interests outside the protagonist.
- **Timeless Heroes compatibility:** external appearance stabilizes at adulthood for every human; chronological ages, 190–250 calendar, children growing up and consequences of injury/illness/death remain. Military command must not treat the protagonist's high chronological age as automatic frailty.

**Practical next implementation:** Source-audit the ten existing `ch12_c79_*` scenes and their choice/effect contracts; deepen the presently short 235–250 three-theater consolidation into coherent playable campaigns with 2–4 linked consequential turns per theater where narratively justified, without deleting/rewiring stable saved scenes. Integrate Dragon assignments only after a legitimate King coronation, earned personal relationships, rival initiatives and fiscal/civilian stakes; add alternate tactical choices and explicit fail/retreat results. Preserve 8 ending categories, original ending manuscripts, 12 chapters, and save compatibility. **Status APPROVED_DESIGN ONLY in HTML v0.4.79: not implemented/QA tested.**

### IMPLEMENTATION / TEST PLAN (REQUIRED LATER, NOT CLAIMED DONE)

1. Source-of-truth age audit: `state.age`, date advancement, save/load/validSave, per-NPC age/birth year anchors, AI/narrative descriptions, health/mortality and ending text. Implement visual/biological-age presentation separately from chronological age; preserve old saves and 16-year-old start; test across 190–250 and minors growing into adults.
2. Add organic Inner Circle recruitment, history, role/office separation, mutual NPC relations and exit consequences, without automatic entry for any historical figure. Respect map, age, rank, loyalty and discovery.
3. After earned King title, offer founding and appointment scenes for Five Dragon Generals (0–5), record recipient identities, consent/acceptance, eligibility and independent missions; provide wartime callbacks through imperial unification, voluntary no-Dragons and remain-King routes.
4. Regression tests: eight endings, adult romance/consent, family children and succession, protagonist frontline freedom, NPC ages/meeting/social etiquette, independence and late-period deaths; actual offline `file://` persistent save/reload remains **UNVERIFIED** in v0.4.79 and must not be claimed otherwise.

## WB-01P — v0.4.79 ACTIVE CHECKPOINT — THE MANDATE OF UNIFICATION (11 Nov 2026)

**New binding decision:** The player explicitly requires an optional ending as **Emperor who unified China**, not merely a ruler of the River Marches. Eight main ending types remain; E01 now renders as either (1) historically earned regional monarchy or (2) genuinely earned unified imperial state. ## WB-01G — v0.4.70 ACTIVE CHECKPOINT — NARRATIVE AGES AND BIOLOGICAL FAMILY (11 Nov 2026)

**Latest explicit user direction:** All six existing fictional romance candidates retain **fixed adult ages**, allowing biological pregnancy to be a deliberate available narrative route; show stronger adult chemistry with the wife and possible concubines. The user's requested graphic sexual acts were **not authored**; story scenes use consent, kisses, intimate adult dialogue and discreet private metaphor/fade-to-black. This deliberately overrides the prior assumption that unknown, chronologically advancing candidate ages foreclose biological childbirth, but **does not change** the 16-year-old protagonist's normal aging, the 18+ adult/consent gate, or historical NPC chronology.

**Fixed fictional ages, non-historical convention:** Shen Lian **29**, Lin Yue **26**, He Qing **28**, Cai Zhen **27**, Lan **25**, Song Wan **30**. These **do not increment over campaign years** and are shown only in People after actual discovery. The numbers are an intentionally ahistorical simplification solely for the *six original romance women*; do not claim biological timelessness is realistic historical demographics or medical evidence. Their career, known achievements, years of relationship, geographic placement and memory of events still continue across years. Do not assign these ages to any newly added historical female NPC or apply this convention to the protagonist.

**Actual implementation:** `ALL_UNDER_HEAVEN_v0.4.70_DEV_FIXED_ROMANCE_AGE_BIOLOGICAL_FAMILY.html`, SHA-256 `8c9e49f85c69c0f27620f46435a6c7a7ed512510d3ceb3b3ddeff84482d8ee28`. **14 newly registered scenes** relative to 388: 12 character-specific private encounters (six women × 225 and 230 CE) plus **two birth/child-care callbacks** (227 for a child born 226, 232 for a child born 231). The 225 private opportunity branches from the existing household council; the 230 private opportunity branches from the existing in-person home encounter. Only the principal wife or a concubine already in the household, with an adult protagonist, can appear. Both a purely affectionate evening without children and a mutual decision to consider a biological child are supported; either can be declined. The birth must be selected at the later 227/232 household window and was **not** conferred by an earlier private scene. Dialogue and metaphors are distinct per candidate. The existing 229 voluntary guardianship/foster route remains separately available and distinct from biological birth.

**New state (additive / legacy safe):** `auh70_plan_225`, `auh70_plan_230`, `auh70_evening_225`, `auh70_evening_230`, `auh70_birth_decided_227`, `auh70_birth_decided_232`, `auh70_family_consulted`, `auh70_bio_children` list of `{born,mother,kind:'biological',welcomed}`. `children_present` is set only after a specifically witnessed birth choice. Biological records carry birth year and distinct mother; the primary wife remains exactly one, concubines at most five, and a previously fostered child is not rewritten as biological. Older local save keys, scene IDs and eight ending manuscript IDs remain unchanged. New children appear in the Character panel and the relevant mother’s People observations after they exist in state, and receive limited callbacks in the late ledger and epilogue bridges. Existing guardianship UI now explicitly says **Foster child**, not **First child**.

**Targeted QA actually performed:** Node JS syntax **PASS**; Chromium with a generated, valid initial save and prepared 225/227/230/232 household checkpoint **PASS**; **36 configuration cases** (six individual wives + 30 wife/concubine ordered pairs), **six-person home**, adult/no-wife gates, no child from intimacy alone, no pregnancy after decline, eight ending registries **PASS**. Real click trail 390px mobile and 1280px desktop traversed adult Lin Yue scene → planned child → 227 family birth choice, with valid state, no page errors or horizontal overflow. Another prepared-state route produced two biological children with different mothers in separate years. **402 registered scenes, 25 People, eight manuscript bridges**.

**Not certified:** Full organic 190→218 marriage→concubines→225 or 230 child birth→all eight endings; native file-origin persistent localStorage/export/import; comprehensive chronology coherence after the deliberate fixed-age exception; all eight ending reachability, political sovereignty throughout, and independently nuanced family aftermath for every possible mixed household. These remain development tasks, not completed release claims.

## WB-01F — v0.4.69 ACTIVE CHECKPOINT — FAMILY LIFE AND VOLUNTARY GUARDIANSHIP (11 Nov 2026)

**Latest user request / continuation:** Build lived household life after the one-wife / optional-consenting-concubine system without undermining player-led geopolitics, direct military command, historical NPC independence or romance. The player and wife can welcome the child with witnessed kin consent or support the relatives without taking the child into their household. Alternative 229/232 regional-kingdom anchors are supported. Original political options, 225–234 year transitions and frontline leadership remain accessible.

All **388 scenes, 25 People and eight ending-block registrations** verified by runtime count. **NOT VERIFIED:** complete organic Prologue→spouse→229 guardianship→all eight ending passages, native file-origin persistent browser save/export, detailed historical household/kinship law and comprehensive outcome causality.

**Next:** Ensure branch-specific inherited household/economic policy, consequences of moving the household in war, child as an autonomous person at later ages, additional credible earlier parenthood opportunities if chronology warrants, all endings' own lasting relationship/household effects and stronger historical NPC reactions. Do not automatically declare an adopted child a dynastic heir.

## WB-01E — v0.4.68 PREVIOUS CHECKPOINT — ONE WIFE AND OPTIONAL CONCUBINES (11 Nov 2026)

**Latest binding user correction:** The protagonist may have **ONE principal wife only**. Other established romance candidates may individually choose to join as **concubines**, NOT second wives. Being a ruler does not grant an NPC’s affection or household status. Formal standing and household expectations for a wife and a concubine are differentiated; individual speech, resources, prior relationship, property and right to refuse must remain respected. After voluntary handovers, spouses/concubines entering domestic life focus on homemaking and potential future child care, never compulsory childbirth.

Age remains **16 at 190 CE**; new household opportunities occur only after adulthood and earned marriage (218 or 223 CE). Six distinct time windows are attached to existing scenes: **223, 225, 227, 229, 230, 232 CE**, one additional concubine maximum per time window and five total across a lifetime. The initial 223 window only makes sense after an earlier marriage and earned family time; a 223 marriage may leave the later five windows. **Core state:** Existing `flags.auh66_spouse` remains the sole wife, with legacy status unchanged. New `flags.auh68_concubines` (unique ID array, max five), `auh68_concubine_years`, `auh68_concubine_roles`, `auh68_concubine_handover`, `auh68_windows_used`, `auh68_visit_year`, `auh68_return_scene`, `auh68_principal_consented`, and `auh68_household_care` track independent choice, informed household discussion and continuity. No `auh68_second_wife` or second-wife role is created. The abandoned intermediate work-in-progress two-wife concept is **NOT CANON / NOT USED**. **Do not** migrate an existing spouse into concubine status automatically. Older v0.4.66/v0.4.67 marriage saves retain their spouse and prior work agreements; new choices are optional.

**QA performed:** Node inline JavaScript check PASS. Chromium state/choice tests **30 wife/concubine pair permutations (6 × 5)** PASS, an actual sequential **one wife + five concubines** case across 223/225/227/229/230 PASS, refusals/underage/unmet status/previously platonic/neglected household/no marriage/old save negative gates PASS, eight ending manuscript registrations PASS. Real UI trails: **8 button clicks at mobile 390 px and 8 at desktop 1280 px**, without JS errors or horizontal overflow, both preserving one principal wife. **Limits:** Checkpoints are artificially prepared from plausible previously earned flags, NOT a complete 190–234 full organic run; physical `file://` localStorage and multi-generation household life not certified; the six historical NPC relation arcs and eight ending reachability remain partially unverified. The exact social/legal standing across alternate regimes needs continued historical review; do not claim this prototype simulates a full Han household law code.

Do not replace substantial Three Kingdoms politics or frontline player agency with repetitive household bookkeeping.

## WB-01D — v0.4.67 PREVIOUS CHECKPOINT (11 Nov 2026)

**Newest user ruling (BINDING for future work):** In **new marriages**, a wife no longer has an external paid/public profession; by **her own explicit agreement**, she leaves that occupation and concentrates on maintaining the family home and raising children **if children are later freely chosen and actually occur**. The game should not narrate an automatic pregnancy, child, new property title or employment. It is a home-life preference, not permission to coerce an NPC or erase her judgment and history. Unmarried women continue their lives and occupations. A protagonist who marries still must supply resources, be present when possible, and help safeguard the family during military and political crises. Historical era and household social status should affect arrangements rather than simply relabeling all women as interchangeable homemakers.

**Code:** `ALL_UNDER_HEAVEN_v0.4.67_DEV_HOUSEHOLD_LIFE_AFTER_MARRIAGE.html` (source SHA-256: `dc70ef3325249d15323690c207a1538925d8190faca1b4b5012bc7a683aec7fb`). Married NPCs are not accidentally shown managing the old granary, ferry, infirmary, court petitions, stage contracts or caravan in **222 CE / 230 CE**. Their scenes take place at their actual shared home after the protagonist travels there. The People panel reflects current household role and former career; Character overview shows spouse/year and homemaker status, and each ending bridge acknowledges home life without changing existing ending IDs.

**16 additional scene records:** six `ch_c67_settle_{shen,linyue,heqing,caizhen,lan,songwan}` scenes for 218, six corresponding `ch11_c67_settle_*` scene aliases for 223, `ch10_c67_home_219` (in-person post-frontier return), `ch12_c67_home_224` (family security before campaigning), plus legacy-agreement discussions `ch11_c67_reconsider_223` and `ch12_c67_reconsider_230`. New flags `auh67_home_role='homemaker'`, `auh67_handover_completed`, `auh67_home_from_year`, `auh67_family_intent`, `auh67_219_home`, `auh67_224_home`, `auh67_legacy_discussed`; `auh66_household='wife_at_home'` for new consensual marriages. Family intent is hope or no specific child promise, **never an implicit child**.

**Legacy-save continuity:** Old v0.4.66 saves with a married spouse expressly allowing external work retain `auh66_household='mutual_work_and_separate_obligations'` and are not silently rewritten. An in-person 223 / 230 discussion offers a **mutually agreed change** to full-time homemaking, or retention of the original promise. Saves already beyond those checkpoints do not fabricate that conversation. Earlier marriage and ending flags/IDs remain valid.

**Actual QA:** HTML inline JavaScript parsed in Node; Chromium source-loaded targeted tests passed **12 marriage paths** (six candidates × two years), with correct Chapter X/XI alias continuations, full-time homemaker flag, family plan flag and `validSave` state. Legacy pact before/after optional renegotiation, unmarried path, 224 household option, 230 married-in-person conditional prose and all eight ending scene/bridge registrations were checked. **3 actual Chromium button trails**, each **5 clicks** (218 Lin Yue on 390px and 1280px; 223 He Qing on 390px), traversed proposal → wedding → household settlement → first evening → original campaign, no JavaScript error / viewport overflow. **No full organic Prologue-to-marriage route QA, no native file-origin persistent-storage verification, and no full eight-ending reachability certification.**

## WB-01B — v0.4.65 PREVIOUS CHECKPOINT (11 Nov 2026)

**Source of implementation truth:** `ALL_UNDER_HEAVEN_v0.4.65_DEV_EARNED_RIVER_KINGDOM.html` SHA-256 `a388794e6d63f47dd374334194ba241d2756b4282c297b1ebeccb791b298b7be`. Exactly **10 new** scenes with IDs `ch12_c65_01_*`–`ch12_c65_10_*`, entered only by a newly chosen, eligible path from the Chapter XII 225 CE council. **306 scenes; 25 People, including 15 historical; 8 unchanged main ending manuscripts; six adult female romance candidates**. No new historical NPC, romance identity or save schema. World Bible v1.56 WB-01A now describes the **previous** v0.4.64 state, not the current absence of a crown path.

**Earned kingdom, NOT an automatic empire:** The protagonist can voluntarily seek a sovereign regional kingdom spanning multiple river jurisdictions through 225 petition and chosen foundation, the existing 227–228 frontier campaign, an actual 229 territorial settlement, the existing 230 in-person romance visit/skip and 232 council, a public charter and a 233 defended or treaty-secured border. Player may accept the regional crown, decline it, withdraw after failure, or abdicate later. `auh65CanConvene`, `auh65Holding`, `auh65FrontierResolved`, `auh65Eligible` and `auh65Crowned` require recorded foundations. Source stores `auh65_territory_held`, `auh65_council_approved`, `auh65_frontier_secured`, `auh65_crown_accepted` and only then `sovereign_title_earned` plus `sovereign_claim_ratified`. Merely holding a local district, pressing a claim, rising in rank or achieving an ambition score is **insufficient**. King of the River Marches is a **regional**, politically contested title, **not an imperial throne over China**. E01 now has an authored feasible chain from a prior earned 225 state, not an eight-ending full-game certification.

**Gameplay consequences:** Military path allows personal frontline leadership, but requires effective preceding campaigns. Civic route may unite jurisdictions by witnessed law and reliable frontier negotiation without requiring a personal duel. Officials, garrisons and households have independent vetoes and material needs; failed or coerced claims stay unratified. Country-level geography and all major historical-ruler survival are **not yet** fully recomputed for the expanded alternative history. Crown alters `auh63SocialPosition`, later council prose, the 234 ledger and `auh64DecideEnding`. Actual recorded death E08 still overrides a living royal ending. An explicit council abdication invalidates reigning E01 without deleting past history. Earlier original Chapter XII choices and eight bridges/ending manuscripts are untouched. The 230 in-person romance and 234 final-council battle remain in sequence, not cut out.

**DEEP-targeted QA:** `node --check` on inline game JavaScript: PASS. Chromium source-loaded JS tests: **10 defined post-225 scenarios**, including armed success, civilian/treaty success, decline, contested consent, failed expansion, unearned claim, refusal, abdication and border failure: **PASS** with `validSave` and `bridge_E01` only on eligible cases. Additional old-save no-expansion 232 choice-count check PASS; two Chromium actual-click trails (**25 clicks each at 390 px and 1280 px**) reached 234 with validated regional crown and correct rank, **zero console page errors or horizontal overflow**. These are controlled, *earlier-founded 225 state fixtures*, **not a complete organic Prologue-to-E01 playthrough**. Native `file://` offline persistence and real device export/import remain UNVERIFIED.

**Next high-priority risks:** full Prologue→claimed territories→E01 route audit, local offline persistence, dynamic named regional-control map and NPC/historical post-divergence reactions, reconciliation of eight ending manuscripts with specific romances and lifelong households, late-life mortality beyond controlled endings. Keep progression earned and child/partner ages consistent. Development file remains an offline-first self-contained HTML, not a certified release.

## WB-00 — READ FIRST / AI NAVIGATION

**Priority order:** latest explicit user decision > Master GDD v1.28 design rules > actual tested v0.4.75 HTML behavior for implementation > this Bible's maintained continuity and planning > dated archival statements. For any code change, recheck the actual build; documentation is not executable proof.

**Read policy:** ALWAYS read WB-00 and WB-01 first. Then consult **only** the applicable sections below. Do not ingest the 315 KB source archive or the 2.3 MB HTML wholesale to change one scene. Find the scene ID in the HTML and inspect its incoming/outgoing dependencies.

| Task | Required sections after WB-00 / WB-01 | Where to inspect code |
|---|---|---|
| Chapter VI–VII scene / historical actor | WB-02 chronology; WB-03 historical cast; WB-05 specific chapter card; WB-07 event anchor | Affected `ch06_XX` / `ch07_XX`, immediately preceding/following scene, flagged callback helpers |
| NPC introduction / personality / portrait | WB-03; relevant WB-04 for romance; WB-08 QA | `PEOPLE`, `DISCOVERY_SCENES`, `PORTRAITS`, encounter flag |
| Romance / friendship / household | WB-04; WB-06 relevant design; chapter card | Gated flags, actual choices, following-scene callback, save schema |
| Political/war historical scene | WB-02; WB-07; WB-05 relevant chapter card | Local scene and affected battle/office/faction world state |
| Save/calendar/engine | WB-01; WB-02; WB-08 | `storyClock`, save/import/export, `setLifetimeYear`, `validSave` and actual route fixtures |
| Ending or secret manuscript work | WB-05 ending contract; **archive only as needed** | `LIFETIME_DRAFT.endingBlocks`, actual terminal arbitration and testable route |
| Simple prose/copyedit | Relevant chapter card and 1–3 surrounding scenes | FAST targeted regression; no chapter-I replay |

**Status vocabulary:** `LOCKED_DESIGN` = agreed rule, `IMPLEMENTED` = observed code, `TARGETED_QA_PASS` = scoped test evidence, `PARTIAL/UNVERIFIED` = unfinished proof, `ARCHIVE_ONLY` = historical snapshot, `PROPOSED` = not approved/implemented as runtime. No inference that 12 chapter cards means 12 polished chapters or that an ending manuscript is reachable.

**Active file contract:** exactly three routine files: Master GDD v1.28 FAST working edition (underlying approved design unchanged), this World Bible v1.64 FAST, and offline HTML v0.4.72. Older versions remain archival checkpoints. Archive ZIP consulted for rare traceability; no recurring project workspace/git or ancillary source bundle required.

- **NEW BINDING STARTING AGE (11 November 2026; v0.4.63 IMPLEMENTED / TARGETED_QA_PASS):** All **new campaigns begin at age 16 in 190 CE**; there is **no starting-age picker**. The player still chooses a name and two exceptional talents. The year-precision `storyClock` advances approximately **17 at 191, 19 at 193, 34 at Red Cliffs (208), 46 in 220, 60 by 234**, without fabricated birthday month/day. **Social protocol:** a sixteen-year-old unranked applicant is addressed as a junior by veteran officers, while service, witness-backed authority, command, charter, council role and real troop support change respect and address across later chapters. Historically famous NPCs retain their own ranks and motives; age alone never guarantees or blocks leadership or combat. 14 existing scenes receive added/adjusted contextual treatment (early Shen Lian, Liu Bei, Zhang Fei, Guan Yu, council; later Red Cliffs Liu Bei/Zhuge Liang/Zhao Yun/Zhang Fei; Chapter XI/ XII). Character panel derives **Position / Command, Affiliation, Basis of Authority** from actual tracked flags rather than displaying an outdated generic Retainer rank. Historical People roles are labelled **first-known**, not claimed to stay unchanged for four decades. Early-190 Shen choices allow only age-appropriate goodwill; adult courtship and intimacy gates remain age 18+ and consensual. **Existing valid v0.4.62 or older saves preserve their original chosen starting age, current age, calendar and scene**, and are not silently retconned; restart creates a new age-16 campaign. Existing save key/schema, all 289 scenes, 25 NPCs, six romance identities and eight drafted endings preserved. **QA:** Node `--check`, checkpoint logic initial/191/193/208/220/234, younger farewell, older-save preservation, and real browser character-creation plus earned-standing views at 390 and 1280 px PASS; no page JS errors or overflow. Native file-backed save persistence and full age/rank prose audit across all 289 scenes remain **UNVERIFIED**.
- **NEW LOCKED CREATIVE AUTHORITY (10 November 2026):** *Romance of the Three Kingdoms* is inspiration, not mandatory canon; the player character—not Liu Bei, Zhuge Liang, Zhou Yu or any historical figure—is the principal historical agent. He may lead and decide the greatest campaigns, surpass or defeat any historical figure, claim political supremacy or choose another life, as long as earned through consistent causal world state. No automatic famous-character plot armor or mandatory historical victory. Applies to ALL chapters; implementation coverage is staged and tracked in the matrix below.
